Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The rooms variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 904 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 576 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the rooms decomposition. By minimality, the element a^904 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 576-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the rooms object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 599. The moduli space has dimension at most 284 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by rooms generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 599, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 284. QED.
